| 2026-06-10 | **[SESSION 46 / ADR-0021 Incremental Delta Materiality — GG-025 architectural fix, ADR-0020 D2 superseded]** DS overrode GG-025's post-G6 deferral: "We don't want a band-aid — the architecture needs the correct fix... the recommended values cannot be arbitrary and... [must be] situationally agnostic to execute on edge cases — but structurally sound to not fall apart in the aggregate." **The fix:** materiality is now judged on `delta_p_incr = p_t4 − p_t4_baseline` where `p_t4_baseline = p_exceeds_threshold(0, threshold, pace×48)` — both terms from the same raw-Poisson model, so the p_t4-vs-p_t1 calibration gap (mean 0.376 at tip-off) cancels algebraically rather than being suppressed by a fitted constant. Improvement over the GG-025 registered design: no stored game-start snapshot — baseline is deterministic from pregame pace (pts_l10_mean/36), stateless and restart-safe (G4's mid-game T4 process death would have corrupted a snapshot design). **The gate:** `|delta_p_incr| ≥ max(T4_DELTA_SIGMA_Z·σ_null(t), T4_MIN_DELTA_FLOOR)` where σ_null(t) is the EXACT null std-dev of delta_p_incr at elapsed time t, computed by Poisson pmf enumeration. By Poisson additivity E[delta_p_incr]=0 under the null (verified to 1e-9). Structural invariants tested: σ_null(tip-off)=0; σ_null(final horn)=√(b(1−b)); monotone non-decreasing (martingale variance). **Constants (zero fitted to G4):** Z=2.0 (EXP-019A house σ-convention); floor=0.04 (mirrors MIN_EDGE_TO_APPROVE, ADR-0020 D1 — sub-economic moves can't trade regardless of significance); GAME_MINUTES_TOTAL=48. Edge cases: pace=0 → σ=0 floor governs; t_rem>48 or <0 clamped; pmf tail bounded at Bernoulli max. **G4 replay (n=2,193):** materiality pass rate 74.6%→9.7%; Q1 89.3%→10.8% (calibration flood gone); Q2 55.2%→6.8%; Q4 1.4%→13.5% (sensitivity GAINED where live signal accumulates — proves bias cancelled, not signal suppressed; fixed 0.50 would have killed both). T4_MIN_DELTA_P removed; T4Signal carries p_t4_baseline/delta_p_incr/sigma_null, all logged to t4_position_trajectories.jsonl for G5 empirical validation. Fire condition unchanged (`is_material AND p_t4 > mip+vig`); exit mechanics (ADR-0019) untouched. Tests: 830/830 (10 new σ_null invariant tests, 5 new gate tests; 2 TestMinBetFloor fixtures updated edge 0.03→0.05 — ADR-0020 MIN_EDGE=0.04 casualty, floor logic itself unchanged). Review trigger: post-G5 replay of logged delta_p_incr vs realized outcomes; if fired signals show no realized edge vs non-fired, escalate to DS (GG-004 standard applies to live signal too). GG-025 RESOLVED. | DS |

| 2026-06-10 | **[SESSION 46 / ADR-0022 T-161 ruling: T4 budget realized-value exit credit — GG-023 RESOLVED]** DS directive: "address the T-161 DS Ruling, we need a reset so the strip runs clean on potentially the last game of the season." **Ruling: option (c), realized-value credit, capped at entry cost.** Rejected: (a) revolving credit — a losing exit would free more capacity than the cash that returned, making gross deployment unbounded in a high-churn losing game and reducing the band budget to a non-constraint (losses bounded only by the daily-loss switch); (b) gross commitment cap (status quo) — the G4 failure: $500 pool pinned at 100% before halftime, 29 exits freed capital, `fired=0` all second half, T-152 evidence halved. **Mechanics:** `deployed = open T4 exposure + realized T4 losses this game`. On exit, `credit = size_usd + min(0, exit_pnl)` (`_exit_budget_credit()`): win → entry cost only (capacity never exceeds the authorized budget — wins don't expand risk authorization); loss → entry cost + pnl (losses permanently consume budget). **Invariants (test-enforced):** aggregate band net loss ≤ t4_band_budget (true capital constraint restored); no starvation (capacity recycles — 100 winning round-trips on a 20-concurrent budget all fire); deployed ≥ 0; restart-lossless. **Restart safety:** `_reconstruct_t4_ledger(portfolio, game_date)` rebuilds the ledger at process start from open pool_isolated exposure + per-exit realized losses (wins don't offset other positions' losses — identical arithmetic to the incremental path; G4's mid-game T4 process death would otherwise have authorized a second full budget). **G5 clean strip:** ledger keyed to game_date — G5 starts at $0.00 deployed automatically; G4's unresolved open positions cannot contaminate G5 capacity; no manual state surgery. **Coherence:** daily-loss kill switch (ADR-0012 net semantics) independent and unchanged — band ledger uses per-exit loss accounting (capital authorization), daily-loss uses net (loss tolerance); both tiers fire independently per CLAUDE.md rule 9 / GG-010. BAND_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED halt unchanged, now triggered by true consumed capital. Review trigger: post-G5, if exhaustion still occurs early with ADR-0021's ~10% fire volume, escalate pool sizing to DS. 13 new tests (test_t161_budget_credit.py); 843/843. ADR-0022 ACCEPTED (amends ADR-0013 ledger semantics only). | DS |

Root cause is server-side Kalshi API congestion at ~22:00 UTC basketball rush hour — T-164 Part 1 (inter-order sleep + retry count) addressed self-generated rate pressure, which was not the binding constraint. G5 confirmed 01:00 UTC off-peak batch = 0% failure rate vs 22:00 UTC = 31% failure rate (unchanged post-T-164). 18:00 UTC = 2pm ET is pre-rush with identical off-peak behavior expected. Also synced `PAPER_T4_FLAT_SIZING=true` to local `cron.conf` (was live on VPS but missing from repo). **(3) T-167 — MC script rewrite (`scripts/run_monte_carlo.py`):** Portfolio mode (default, no flag): reads `paper_portfolio.json`, simulates from committed positions using `model_p`, `direction`, `mid_price_at_entry`, `size_usd` with Bernoulli draws per position. Preview mode (`--preview`): reads pregame JSON (pre-deployment simulation, old default behavior). Scenario flags: `--exclude-failed` (Kalshi-confirmed only), `--yes-only` / `--no-only` (EXP-018 direction split), `--haircut PCT` (T-153 integration point). Constants corrected to production values: `DEFAULT_T1_KELLY=0.25`, `DEFAULT_MIN_EDGE=0.04` (were 0.10/0.02, stale from ADR-0010 era). **(4) DS architectural ruling — no per-player exposure caps:** DS explicitly ruled no `max_player_exposure_usd` or `max_player_pct_of_bankroll` in `_HARD_LIMITS`. Wemby concentration in G5 ($323.33 / 32% of portfolio) is structurally expected at single-game Finals scale. Regular-season dilution (15+ games, 300+ markets) is endogenous to market structure — no explicit per-player cap needed. The architecture is structurally sound; ADR-0005 (live capital framework, Phase 2) is the correct venue for live capital concentration parameters. | DS |

Non-cohort players print no tag — None is the explicit fallback. PAM features were already flowing into LR model inference (47 features: prf_w01–15 + mod_01–32); this change makes the archetype visible at the decision log level, not just the model level. **(3) T4 archetype register wiring (`run_t4_inplay.py`):** `QualitativeSignalStore` imported. `pam_archetype: Optional[dict] = None` field added to `T4RoutingResult` (frozen dataclass — default field placed after all non-default fields). `archetype_register: QualitativeSignalStore | None = None` injected via `T4EventLoop.__init__`, stored as `self._archetype_register`. `on_game_state_event` now calls `self._archetype_register.get_archetype(int(event.player_id), game_date)` at the start of each event evaluation, wrapped in try/except (best-effort; never blocks routing). All non-KILL_SWITCH routing results (FIRE_STAGED, FIRE_BAYESIAN, FIRE_T5, NO_ACTION) are rebuilt with `pam_archetype=_pam`. KILL_SWITCH path intentionally left without pam_archetype — kill switch fires before player context is relevant. Design rationale for best-effort pattern: T4 must never stall on a data-enrichment lookup; archetype is observational context, not a routing input. **(4) GG-027 registered:** PAM wired to T1 (model features + audit log) and T4 (decision log + routing result) only. T2/T3/T5 bands have no archetype wiring. T4 archetype does not affect routing math. EnvironmentClassifier (pressure-context game-environment categorizer — elimination_game, series_momentum, crowd_premium, role_expansion_flag → environment_class HIGH_PRESSURE / NEUTRAL / LOW_STAKES) is not built. QS snapshot dated 2026-05-28 not updated for Finals role changes (e.g., Alvarado role expansion). Combined: LOW severity now; HIGH pre-MLB activation. **(5) T-165 registered (MLB gate):** Full PAM integration scope: (a) EnvironmentClassifier in C2; (b) archetype × environment uncertainty multiplier in C3; (c) dynamic MIN_EDGE_TO_APPROVE per environment class; (d) T2/T3/T5 archetype register wiring. Token cost of building the full scope for NBA Finals not justified at this stage — wired T1 and T4 as registers now; full integration deferred. 852/852 tests pass. | DS |

| 2026-06-12 | **[SESSION 55] T-168 (GG-030 RESOLVED): MC correlated simulation fix; GG-031/032 registered; T-170 (T4 ledger correction) RESOLVED** | Four decisions. **(1) T-168 — `simulate_portfolio()` Poisson correlation fix (`scripts/run_monte_carlo.py`, GG-030 RESOLVED):** T-167's MC rewrite (SESSION 53) fixed data sourcing (reads committed positions from `paper_portfolio.json`) but regressed the simulation's correlation structure. It drew independent Bernoulli per position (`rng.random(n_sims) < b["p_win"]`), treating threshold ladders on the same player as independent events. They are not — Wemby's NO ≥35, NO ≥40 both resolve against the same game score. Preview mode (`--preview`) already drew correlated results correctly via Poisson simulation. Fix: `simulate_portfolio()` now matches preview mode. One Poisson score drawn per player per simulation. Lambda inferred via `_infer_lambda()` — least-squares fit to the player's `(threshold, model_p)` pairs using `scipy.optimize.minimize_scalar` with bounds (0.1, 80), where `model_p = P(score ≥ threshold | lambda)` regardless of bet direction. Direction-invariant: `_infer_lambda()` always uses raw `b["model_p"]` — the first implementation used `1.0 - b["model_p"]` for NO bets, inverting the Poisson distribution and causing NO bets with model_p=0.001 (99.9% p_win) to infer an extremely high lambda, reversing all win/loss flags (MC output: "avg bets won: 0.0, P(profit): 0.0%"). Fixed: always use raw model_p. Governance footer updated: GG-030 resolved note replaces "independent Bernoulli" disclaimer. **(2) GG-031 registered (GG-028 blast radius understated):** SESSION 53 and 52 documented 2 duplicated tickers from GG-028. Direct VPS audit of all 41 positions (this session) found 9 duplicated (ticker, direction) pairs — 40% of total deployment ($402.49 doubled exposure), not 2. All 9 follow the same pattern: one `failed: transient` (18:0x UTC primary batch) + one `resting` (01:30 UTC late batch). The "mystery 01:00 UTC trigger" identified in prior sessions is the scheduled 01:30 UTC late PIPE-03 cron window — not a separate trigger. T-166's idempotency fix correctly prevents recurrence. T-169 registered: DS ruling on 9 twins needed — annotate as `duplicate_of` (Option A) or accept as double exposure (Option B). **(3) GG-032 registered (T4 bankroll_usd irreconcilable):** `paper_portfolio_t4.json` header had `bankroll_usd=$1,869.68`, `initial_bankroll_usd=$1,000.00`, `total_realized_pnl=-$21.59`. The gap between bankroll and initial ($869.68 growth) is irreconcilable: position-level P&L sum = -$21.59, G3 pnl=+$39.44, G4 resolved=-$86.69, G4 exited=+$25.66 → true net = -$21.59. Root cause: ADR-0022's `_reconstruct_t4_ledger()` wrote the T1 system bankroll (~$1,869) into T4's `bankroll_usd` field — wrong object, wrong field. True T4 pool value: $500 initial + (-$21.59 realized) = $478.41. Prior claim of "+87% T4 growth" was based on comparing $1,869 bankroll to $1,000 initial — both wrong fields. T-170 registered. **(4) T-170 RESOLVED — T4 ledger corrected on VPS:** `paper_portfolio_t4.json` fields corrected: `bankroll_usd` 1869.68→478.41, `initial_bankroll_usd` 1000.0→500.0. `_t170_correction_note` added to file documenting derivation. Backup written to `logs/paper_portfolio_t4.json.bak_t170` before edit. `total_realized_pnl=-21.59` unchanged (correct). **(5) G5 MC output (correlated simulation):** 41 positions, $1,003.38 deployed, bankroll $130.62. | 2026-06-12 | **[SESSION 58] ADR-0025 ACCEPTED (T-156): Net semantics for daily-loss accumulation formalized; 28 first-ever direct accumulation tests added** | Net-semantics formalization only — no code changes required; existing `_accumulate_daily_loss()` implementation was already correct. ADR-0025 amends ADR-0012 by specifying: `current_daily_loss = max(0, −daily_net_pnl)` where `daily_net_pnl = Σpnl_i` for all trades closed on a calendar day. Kill switch fires when `current_daily_loss ≥ initial_bankroll × MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT`. Cross-day isolation: `_reset_daily_loss_if_new_day()` zeros both fields on day boundary. Threshold anchored to `initial_bankroll_usd`, not current bankroll (prevents shrinking-threshold feedback loop). Motivation: G3 incident — a profitable high-churn night had gross losses $317.76 vs net P&L −$198.84; gross accumulation would have fired the kill switch on a winning session, defeating the purpose of the control. 28 tests across 4 classes: `TestAccumulateDailyLoss` (11 tests: single loss/profit, net offset/full-cover/excess, additive accumulation, no-touch invariants), `TestResetDailyLossIfNewDay` (7 tests: same-day no-op, new-day reset, missing-key edge case, field isolation), `TestCrossDayTransitions` (3 tests: cross-day isolation, carry-forward suppression), `TestKillSwitchThresholdBoundary` (7 tests: constant value, threshold derivation, below/at/above boundary, net recovery, initial-bankroll anchor). 885/885 tests pass. | DS |

| 2026-05-22 | **[RETROACTIVE / T-154] CHANGE_CONTROL.md framework decisions extracted — three-tier classification, SKELETON→ACTIVE ADR requirement, no-emergency-override rule, conditional-language escalation rule** | T-154 governance hygiene pass. (1) **Three-tier change classification (Class A/B/C):** Adopted alongside ADR-0001 (Compass OS). Class A = ADR required + immutable record; Class B = CHANGELOG entry only; Class C = artifact update only. Rationale: changes that affect capital risk, compliance, or model behavior require the full deliberation artifact (ADR); tactical changes require only a record; administrative changes require only state update. (2) **SKELETON→ACTIVE promotion is Class A (ADR required), not Class B:** A band promotion without an ADR makes a live execution path silently active — equivalent to promoting a model version without ADR-0004 controls. Machine-readable `band_status` field in `BAND_REGISTRY` is what separates SKELETON (code runs, no bets) from ACTIVE (bets fire). An ADR is required because this is a capital-risk-bearing state change, not a documentation change. New strategy registration (no live capital) is Class B because no capital-bearing state change occurs at registration time. (3) **No emergency override path for risk limits:** If a risk limit needs urgent adjustment, the correct response is the kill switch (halt all activity) — not a temporary limit override. An emergency override path that bypasses ADR review would create a governance hole that could be used to rationalize risk increases under pressure. No such path was built and none should be. (4) **Conditional language in canonical registers must be escalated before session end — never deferred:** Conditional language ("may be revised", "subject to DS decision") that survives a context window transition looks like a resolved decision to the next context. This was observed in GG-001: a `# 10 concurrent open (stub)` comment survived four gate clearances. The escalation-before-session-end rule closes this class of failure. | DS |

| 2026-06-22 | **[SESSION 69] DS DECISION — Option C (feature-set diagnosis sprint) + DIAGNOSIS FINDINGS: anti-predictive edge driven by missing playoff-context λ multiplier, not model class failure** | **DS decision (Earl Ignacio, 2026-06-22 S69):** T-177 escalation path = **Option C — feature-set diagnosis sprint.** Not Option A (halt + ADR-0004 supersession) and not Option B (direction inversion — flagged as not trivially safe). Rationale: the anti-predictive edge is identifiable in the data as a specific feature engineering gap, making a model class change premature. T-072 (model retrain) may proceed in Phase 2 but targets the feature fix identified below, not a change of model architecture. **Diagnosis findings (n=103 clean bets, NBA Finals G2–G5, conducted S69):** Full feature-set breakdown run on `data/nba_finals_clean_dataset.csv` (is_clean=True). **Finding 1 — Direction asymmetry.** YES bets (n=36, edge>0): win rate 38.9%. NO bets (n=67, edge≤0): win rate 68.7%. YES/NO split is exact (edge>0 ≡ YES direction in this dataset). **Finding 2 — Model vs market calibration error.** YES bets: mean model_p=0.704, mean mip=0.539, actual win rate=0.389. Model error (model_p − actual): +0.315. Market error (mip − actual): +0.150. Market is 2.1× better calibrated on YES bets. NO bets: mean (1−model_p)=0.825, mean (1−mip)=0.693, actual win rate=0.687. Model error: +0.138. Market error: **+0.006** — Kalshi market is essentially perfectly calibrated on NO bets. **Finding 3 — Threshold amplification.** YES bets by threshold: thr=10 (model_p=0.893, mip=0.724, actual=0.643; model_err=+0.250, mkt_err=+0.081); thr=15 (model_p=0.642, mip=0.476, actual=0.250; model_err=+0.392, mkt_err=+0.226); thr=20 (model_p=0.493, mip=0.346, actual=0.222; model_err=+0.271, mkt_err=+0.123). The model's YES error worsens dramatically at thr≥15; at thr=15-20 both model and market overestimate but model is 2-3× worse. **Finding 4 — Implied λ compression.** Inverting model_p to recover the implied Poisson λ: at thr=10, model implies λ=14.0 but actual_pts=12.8 (compression=0.911); at thr=15, model implies λ=16.1 but actual_pts=12.8 (compression=0.792); at thr=20, model implies λ=19.6 but actual_pts=13.8 (compression=0.703). The model systematically assumes scoring rates ~10-30% higher than Finals reality. **Finding 5 — Within-series compression drift.** YES bet win rates by game: G2=71.4% (avg_pts=17.3), G3=25.0% (avg_pts=11.8), G4=41.7% (avg_pts=15.5), G5=22.2% (avg_pts=7.7). Scoring collapses across the series as teams adjust defensive game plans game-by-game. The model cannot update λ within a series; it uses rolling regular-season averages throughout. **Finding 6 — Market efficiency asymmetry.** Kalshi is almost perfectly calibrated on NO bets (+0.006 error) and significantly better than the model on YES bets. The market appears to incorporate Finals-context defensive information that the model's λ does not. **Root cause identified: missing playoff-context multiplier on λ.** The Poisson survival model uses `opp_adj_lambda` calibrated against regular-season opponent defensive ratings. NBA Finals defensive intensity systematically depresses scoring by 0.70–0.91× regular-season λ, with the compression worsening at higher thresholds and deepening across the series. The model has no feature representing playoff round, within-series opponent familiarity, or Finals-specific defensive adjustments. The Kalshi market has this information via informed participant pricing. **This is a feature engineering gap, NOT a model class failure.** The Poisson architecture is appropriate for basketball scoring; the λ input is wrong. **What this finding does NOT prove:** (a) that the anti-predictive pattern will persist in NBA regular season (where no "Finals compression" exists and rolling averages track reality more closely); (b) that the pattern will appear in MLB (different scoring structure). The n=103 sample is a single series with unusual context — ADR-0024 structural adequacy threshold (n≥500, ≥15 opponents) is not met. **Authorized next steps for feature fix (T-072 scope addition):** (i) Add `playoff_round_multiplier` feature to C2 λ derivation — value 1.0 for regular season; to be calibrated from NBA playoff historical data during Phase 2. (ii) Investigate whether `opp_adj_lambda` uses regular-season defensive ratings for Finals opponents — if so, replace with opponent's most recent N playoff games. (iii) Within-series momentum feature: player's scoring in this series vs regular-season rolling average (captures in-series game-plan adjustment). (iv) All three features must be derived from empirical data during Phase 2 retrain — no guessed multipliers. **GG-037 alpha half + GG-004 status unchanged:** diagnosis identifies the likely mechanism but does not close the governance gap; that requires EXP-017 CONFIRMED_POSITIVE at ADR-0024 adequate sample (n≥500/≥15 opponents). T-177 monitor remains active. The DS decision to pursue Option C means that if EXP-017 (MLB or NBA regular season) fires ANTI-PREDICTIVE again after the feature fix is incorporated, the escalation path defaults to Option A (halt + ADR-0004 review). | DS |

Root cause: pandas chunks large CSVs and emits `DtypeWarning` when metadata columns (`train_test_label`, `matchup`, `opponent_team_abbr`, `game_date_dt` — cols 63/64/66/68) have mixed types across chunks. | 2026-06-23 | **[SESSION 79] T-184 COMPLETE — EXP-017 MLB commercial validation script (2 new files, 99 tests, 1609 total green)** | **Script:** `scripts/run_exp017_mlb.py` (new, standalone — no PYTHONPATH needed). **Test suite:** `tests/test_t184_exp017_mlb.py` (new, 99 tests). **Purpose:** EXP-017 MLB is the formal commercial validation experiment for GG-004. It answers whether the MLB LR+Platt model generates positive fee-net EV against actual Kalshi KXMLBHIT prices at ADR-0024-adequate sample size. GG-004 remains OPEN until this script returns CONFIRMED_POSITIVE at Tier 2 criteria. **Script design:** Standalone analysis script (no import from `prediction_market_bot`; scipy optional with chi-squared fallback). Reads `logs/paper_portfolio_mlb.json` (default) or `--csv path` (batch backtest mode). Deduplication: one position per unique `kalshi_ticker` (earliest entry, consistent with EXP-018 method). **Three analysis layers: (1) EV analysis (EXP-018 pattern):** EV/bet, win rate, total P&L, YES/NO direction split. Realized P&L from `pnl` field in positions. `compute_pnl_unit(direction, mip, vig, won)` — consistent with EXP-018. **(2) ADR-0024 tier checks:** Tier 1 (n ≥ 150): EV/bet < -$0.03 → `DIAGNOSTIC_SPRINT` (model class review per ADR-0004). Tier 2 single (n ≥ 500): `CONFIRMED_POSITIVE` (EV>0, p<0.05) or `CONFIRMED_NEGATIVE` (EV<-$0.03). Tier 2 multi (`--nba-n ≥ 150` AND n ≥ 150): same criteria with multi-sport evidence. **(3) Edge monotonicity / T-177 guard (GG-037):** Fisher's exact (scipy) or chi-squared approximation; edge>0 vs edge≤0 win-rate comparison; ANTI-PREDICTIVE / MONOTONIC / INCONCLUSIVE verdict; tercile table (3 rows by fee_net_edge quantile). **EV verdict hierarchy:** `INCONCLUSIVE` (n < 20) → `DIAGNOSTIC_SPRINT` (Tier 1, EV < -$0.03) → `CONFIRMED_POSITIVE`/`CONFIRMED_NEGATIVE` (Tier 2, p < 0.05) → `DIRECTIONAL_POSITIVE`/`DIRECTIONAL_NEGATIVE` (below Tier 2). **MLB-specific threshold breakdown:** EV and win-rate split by k ∈ {1,2,3} (1+ hits, 2+ hits, 3+ hits). k=1 has highest hit rate; different market dynamics than k=3 — breakdown exposes threshold-level edge signals. **`compute_fee_net_edge(direction, model_p, mip):`** YES: `model_p − mip − 0.07×mip×(1−mip)`; NO: `mip − model_p − 0.07×mip×(1−mip)`. Falls back to pre-computed `fee_net_edge` field if present in position (stored by C4). `model_p` is required; positions without it are skipped. **CI mode exit codes:** 0 = positive/INCONCLUSIVE; 2 = CONFIRMED_NEGATIVE; 3 = DIAGNOSTIC_SPRINT; 4 = insufficient data. **MLB position schema contract** (what the MLB paper trading runner must store): `kalshi_ticker`, `direction`, `mid_price_at_entry`, `vig_at_entry`, `model_p`, `threshold` (1/2/3), `outcome` (WIN/LOSS), `pnl` (USD), `game_date`, `player_name`, optionally `fee_net_edge`, `duplicate_of`. **Data status at time of script completion:** MLB paper trading has NOT yet started (Phase 3 is August–September 2026). This script runs on empty portfolio — returns INCONCLUSIVE as expected. First real run: once MLB paper trading accumulates ≥ 20 positions (MIN_N). Tier 1 check (n ≥ 150) expected by mid-August at 2026 season pace. **`constants verified: `ADR_0024_TIER1_N=150`, `ADR_0024_TIER1_DIAG_EV=-0.03`, `ADR_0024_TIER2_N_SINGLE=500`, `ADR_0024_TIER2_N_MULTI=150`, `ADR_0024_P_THRESHOLD=0.05`, `KALSHI_FEE_RATE=0.07`, `MIN_N=20`. **Test suite (99 tests in 12 classes):** `TestKalshiFee` (7), `TestComputeFeeNetEdge` (6), `TestComputePnlUnit` (6), `TestLoadPortfolio` (8), `TestLoadCsv` (5), `TestDeduplicate` (6), `TestEvStats` (4), `TestThresholdBreakdown` (8), `TestAdr0024TierCheck` (11), `TestEvVerdict` (7), `TestEdgeMonotonicity` (8), `TestTercileTable` (4), `TestFisherPvalue` (3), `TestEndToEnd` (11), `TestConstantsAndSportLabel` (5). **Suite progression: 1510 → 1609/1609 green (+99), 0 failures.** VPS deployed (commit following this session). | EL |
| 2026-06-23 | **[SESSION 78] T-183 COMPLETE — MLB C3 model training infrastructure (2 new files, 77 tests, 1510 total green)** | **Script:** `scripts/train_mlb_model.py` (new). **Test suite:** `tests/test_t183_mlb_c3.py` (new, 77 tests). **Architecture decision: sport-agnostic `ModelForecaster` + MLB-specific training script — no new `MLBForecaster` class.** Rationale: C3 is sport-agnostic at the class level per ADR-0031; only C1/C2 are sport-specific. Adding a new class would violate the ADR-0031 claim that C4–C8 require no changes when adding a sport. The training script is the sport-specific component; `ModelForecaster` loads and runs any artifact with the standard `{"pipeline", "feature_names", "col_medians"}` schema. **Target variable design (`_build_mlb_target`):** MLB KXMLBHIT markets have variable thresholds (1+, 2+, 3+ hits). Each game row is expanded to one row per threshold: `target = (h >= k)` as Int8/NA. `threshold` is stored as a float feature in the LR model — one model scores any threshold at inference time. `h` (raw hit count) lives in `_NON_FEATURE_MLB` to prevent data leakage. **`_NON_FEATURE_MLB` frozenset** excludes: identity/metadata columns (`player_id`, `player_name`, `team_abbreviation`, `game_id`, `game_date`, `season`, `season_type`, `matchup`, `opponent_team_abbr`, `snapshot_id`, `feature_run_id`, `feature_version`, `temporal_cutoff`, `train_test_label`), C1 audit columns (`ingestion_run_id`, `ingested_at`), raw box-score outcomes (`ab`, `h`, `doubles`, `triples`, `hr`, `rbi`, `bb`, `k`, `hbp`, `sf`), training artifacts (`y_target`), and temporal helper (`game_date_dt`). Features `is_home_game`, `threshold`, `ba_l10`, `opp_sp_era_l5`, `park_factor_hits`, etc. are NOT in the set. **Shadow gate (ADR-0009):** No prior MLB model exists → baseline is the class prior (null model: always predicts `mean(y_holdout)`). `brier_null = p * (1 - p)`. Gate: `brier_delta = brier_null - brier_model >= 0.001` AND `ece <= 0.05`. Script exits `sys.exit(2)` on shadow gate failure (without `--force-save`). **`HOLDOUT_CUTOFF = "2026-01-01"` fixed before training:** 2026 MLB season data is the holdout. Anti-gaming split — do not change without a superseding entry. **`MIN_TRAIN_SAMPLES = 30`, `MIN_HOLDOUT_SAMPLES = 10`** (pre-expansion; after 3× threshold expansion: ≥90 train, ≥30 holdout minimum). **sklearn Pipeline** matches `forecaster.py:354-358` exactly: `StandardScaler → CalibratedClassifierCV(LR(C=1.0, solver="lbfgs", max_iter=1000, random_state=42, class_weight="balanced"), method="sigmoid", cv=3)`. **`_mlb_model_version(feature_names)`:** `"mlb-v1-" + sha1[:8]` of `"LogisticRegression+PlattScaling\|MLB\|" + ",".join(sorted(feature_names))` — deterministic, order-independent. **Artifact schema** matches NBA artifact loader contract (`run_pregame.py` loads via `joblib.load`): `{"pipeline": pipe, "feature_names": feature_names, "col_medians": col_medians, "model_version": model_version, "thresholds": [1,2,3], "sport": "mlb"}`. **`CALIBRATION_TEMPERATURE["mlb"] = 1.0` (identity):** Confirmed in `calibration.py`; comment: "No MLB resolved data exists yet; toggled once MLB dark-period dataset available (DS directive 2026-06-16)." T-176 (MLB calibration temperature derivation) remains open. **BOT_C4_ACTIVE guard:** `train_mlb_model.py` exits `sys.exit(1)` if `BOT_C4_ACTIVE` is set — training must never run from within a live execution context. **Test contamination root cause and fix:** 10 of 77 tests in `TestEndToEndDryRun` passed in isolation but failed in the full 1510-test suite with "FATAL: BOT_C4_ACTIVE is set." Root cause: `tests/test_t3_integration.py` imports `from prediction_market_bot.run_paper_trade import T3_CONFIRM_MULTIPLIER, T3_FADE_MULTIPLIER`, which triggers `run_paper_trade.py:59`: `os.environ["BOT_C4_ACTIVE"] = "true"` at module import time — contaminating all downstream tests in the pytest session. Fix: `autouse=True` fixture on `TestEndToEndDryRun` that calls `monkeypatch.delenv("BOT_C4_ACTIVE", raising=False)` before each test in that class. Pattern documented: BOT_C4_ACTIVE guards belong only on scripts that invoke C4 (`run_paper_trade.py`, `run_t4_live.py`); the env-var set-at-import in `run_paper_trade.py` is a side-effect that test isolation must account for. **Test suite (77 tests in 11 classes):** `TestBuildMlbTarget` (13), `TestComputeEce` (5), `TestImpute` (4), `TestMlbModelVersion` (5), `TestExtractFeatureMatrix` (8), `TestNonFeatureSet` (10), `TestNullBaseline` (3), `TestShadowGateLogic` (6), `TestModelArtifact` (4), `TestEndToEndDryRun` (14), `TestMultipleThresholds` (5). **Suite progression: 1433 → 1510/1510 green (+77), 0 failures. No VPS behavior change (train_mlb_model.py is an offline training script; no runner wires it yet).** | EL |
| 2026-06-23 | **[SESSION 77] T-182 COMPLETE — MLB C2 baseball feature set (2 new files, 60 tests, 1433 total green)** | **Module:** `src/prediction_market_bot/c2_feature_store/mlb_features.py` (new). **Test suite:** `tests/test_t182_mlb_c2.py` (new, 60 tests). **C1 patch:** `mlb_ingestion.py` `_ip_to_decimal()` added — MLB Stats API "inningsPitched" uses baseball notation ("5.2" = 5 innings + 2 outs = 5⅔ = 5.667 decimal); this fix prevents a systematic short-outing of pitcher stats. **Feature version:** `MLB_FEATURE_VERSION = "1.0.0"` per ADR-0004 policy (separate from NBA 1.4.0). **TI-01 compliance:** all rolling features computed via `.shift(1).rolling(min_periods=MIN_PERIODS)` — current game box score NEVER enters its own feature vector; `temporal_cutoff = game_date of prior game` (NaT for first game of player log). **Rolling batter features (L10 window, min_periods=3):** `ba_l10`, `slg_l10`, `obp_l10`, `ops_l10`, `h_l10_mean`, `k_rate_l10`, `bb_rate_l10`, `hr_rate_l10`. **Sum-then-divide pattern:** BA/SLG/OBP use Σ(h)/Σ(ab) rolling sums rather than mean of per-game rates — eliminates bias from partial-AB games (a 0-for-1 games receives the same denominator weight as a 4-AB game in the per-game-rate approach). **Context features:** `days_rest` (days since prior game), `b2b_flag` (days_rest < 2). **Park factors:** static 30-venue dict `_PARK_FACTOR_HITS` keyed by `venue_name` string from game_starters; Coors Field=1.120 (extreme hitter's park), Oracle Park=0.970 (pitcher-friendly); unknown venue→default 1.000. **Opponent context:** `opp_runs_allowed_l10` from `MLBTeamStatsStore.get_opp_runs_allowed()` (L10, min_periods=3, TI-01). **Starting pitcher overlay (MANDATORY):** SP identified via `game_starters[game_id]` — home batter gets away SP, away batter gets home SP. SP features: `opp_sp_era_l5`, `opp_sp_k9_l5`, `opp_sp_bb9_l5` from `MLBPitcherStatsStore.get_*_l5(sp_id, game_date)` (L5 starts, min_periods=2, TI-01). When sp_id is None or SP has < 2 prior starts: all SP features=None, `opp_sp_is_known=0.0`. K9/BB9 computed via total K/BB ÷ total IP (same sum-then-divide discipline as batter rates — avoids per-start rate bias from short outings). **Metadata:** `feature_run_id = f"C2MLB-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"`, `feature_version = "1.0.0"`, `train_test_label = None` (populated by `assign_train_test_labels()`). **C6 audit trail:** 5 event types — `mlb_feature_derivation_start`, `mlb_feature_derivation_complete`, `mlb_feature_derivation_error`, `temporal_integrity_warning` (fires when any player has NaT temporal_cutoff), `mlb_train_test_split_applied`. `ok=False` returned and error event logged if C6 logger.log() returns falsy (logger failure path). **Train/test split:** `assign_train_test_labels(feature_df, train_end_date, val_end_date)` — rows with NaT `temporal_cutoff` → `train_test_label=None` (TI-02 excluded from splits); others: ≤train_end→TRAIN, ≤val_end→VALIDATION, else→TEST. **Test suite (60 tests in 5 classes):** `TestIpToDecimal` (11 — full notation coverage including edge cases), `TestParkFactorTable` (4 — extremes, full-range, unknown-venue guard), `TestComputeRollingBatterFeatures` (16 — TI-01 correctness, first/few-game NaN, BA/SLG/OPS/OBP values, days_rest, b2b_flag, window boundary), `TestComputeFeaturesBulk` (23 — IF-C1-C2 contract, SP known/unknown/insufficient, park factor known/unknown, team store, C6 audit, logger failure, feature_run_id prefix, temporal_integrity_warning, train_test_label=None), `TestAssignTrainTestLabels` (5 — chronological split, NaT exclusion, boundary inclusive, audit log). **Key testing lesson:** `pytest.approx()` does not compose correctly with pandas `.all()` — `(series == pytest.approx(value)).all()` fails silently. Fix: use `np.allclose(series.values, value)` for float comparisons, direct equality for exact values. **1373→1433 total tests (+60), 0 failures. No VPS behavior change (C2 is a new module; no runner wires it yet).** | EL |

| 2026-06-24 | **[SESSION 81 cont.] MLB Stats API IP-blocking finding — VPS blocked for historical seasons; generate_mlb_features.py must run locally; PIPE-05 feature refresh design constraint registered** | **Finding (SESSION 81 — T-187B setup):** During T-187B execution on VPS (157.230.91.33 / DigitalOcean), the MLB Stats API (`statsapi.mlb.com`) returned HTTP 406 for all historical season endpoints (2024, 2025) but HTTP 200 for the current season (2026). This is CDN/IP-based access control: DigitalOcean cloud IPs are blocked from accessing historical MLB data. Confirmed via: (1) `GET /api/v1/sports/1/players?season=2024` → 406 from VPS, 200 from local machine. (2) `GET /api/v1/people/{id}/stats?season=2024` → 406 from VPS. (3) Browser User-Agent header makes no difference (406 regardless). (4) 2026 season data: accessible from VPS (HTTP 200). Root cause: `_fetch_position_players(season)` calls `resp.raise_for_status()` which raises on 406, caught by exception handler which returns `[]`; cached empty list survives as bad cache. Players for 2024/2025 were cached as `[]` before issue was discovered. **Resolution:** (1) Killed VPS process. (2) Deleted bad cache files (`players_2024.json`, `players_2025.json`). (3) Generate full 3-season feature matrix locally (all seasons accessible from local machine). (4) SCP output CSV to VPS → run `train_mlb_model.py` on VPS. **Architectural constraint established:** MLB Stats API historical data (season < current) requires local/non-cloud IP to access. VPS can ONLY access current-season data. **PIPE-05 feature refresh design:**  Model training must occur locally (or from an unblocked IP). Daily VPS feature refresh for inference must use `--seasons 2026` only (current season accessible). Implication: `exp_output/MLB/mlb_feature_matrix.csv` on VPS starts as a 3-season locally-generated file; daily VPS refresh with `--seasons 2026` will overwrite it with 2026-only features over time. This is acceptable for inference quality (most-recent features matter for betting); the initial 3-season data is only needed for model training. For annual retraining, local generation + SCP upload must be repeated. **No ADR required:** this is an infrastructure/deployment constraint, not an algorithm decision. Registered as known operational constraint for PIPE-05 runbook. | EL |

| 2026-06-24 | **[SESSION 81 / T-187B] Per-threshold MLB LR+Platt models: pooled model ECE failure root cause, architecture fix, and t=3 exclusion from active scope** | **Root cause of ECE=0.1246 FAIL on pooled model:** A single `CalibratedClassifierCV(LR, class_weight="balanced", cv=3)` trained on all three thresholds pooled together cannot produce well-calibrated per-threshold probabilities. `class_weight="balanced"` forces equal per-class representation across all threshold values, inflating the effective positive rate to ~26% (pooled average). But per-threshold actual rates diverge dramatically: t=1 actual=54.5%, t=2 actual=18.4%, t=3 actual=3.9%. Platt scaling (Logistic sigmoid) fitted on the pooled distribution calibrates to the 26% average and cannot correct per-threshold systematic biases simultaneously. Result: at holdout evaluation, t=1 model predicts 73.3% (actual 54.5%, over-predicts +18.8pp); t=2 model predicts 35.4% (actual 18.4%, over-predicts +17.0pp). ECE = 0.1246 >> 0.05 threshold. **Diagnostic run (local — VPS OOM on full CSV):** Per-threshold Platt ECE verified by running separate pipelines on each threshold's subset. Results: t=1 ECE=0.013 (PASS ≤0.05), t=2 ECE=0.011 (PASS), t=3 ECE=0.004 (PASS). Brier delta: t=1=+0.0055 (PASS ≥0.001), t=2=+0.0022 (PASS), t=3=+0.0002 (FAIL — below threshold). **Architecture fix:** Per-threshold separate `StandardScaler → CalibratedClassifierCV(LR, sigmoid, cv=3)` pipelines for t=1 and t=2. Each pipeline trains on its own threshold's rows only; Platt scaling calibrates to the correct per-threshold base rate. `threshold` is removed from the feature vector — it no longer varies within a per-threshold pipeline and would be a degenerate constant column. `col_medians` are shared (computed from the full `train_df` before splitting by threshold). Shadow gate: BOTH t=1 AND t=2 must PASS for the artifact to be saved. **t=3 exclusion (GG-042 registered):** At t=3, base rate=3.9% and AUC=0.61 produce a Brier improvement of only +0.0002 (required ≥0.001). Even if the gate passed, t=3 bets would almost never fire — the model predicts ~3.9% while Kalshi asks ~8¢ → edge is consistently negative. t=3 is excluded from `THRESHOLDS` pending T-187G (live SP overlay injection, which would provide meaningful signal for rare 3+ hit events). `_EXCLUDED_THRESHOLDS = [3]` registered and GG-042 logged. **Artifact schema change:** Old: `{"pipeline": pipe, ...}`. New: `{"pipelines": {1: pipe1, 2: pipe2}, "feature_names": [...18 features...], "col_medians": medians, "model_version": model_version, "thresholds": [1, 2], "sport": "mlb"}`. `_mlb_model_version(feature_names)` hash unchanged (features identical across thresholds). **Inference change:** `run_mlb_paper_trade.py` dispatches `pipelines[int(mkt.threshold)].predict_proba(X)[0, 1]`. `threshold` removed from `_build_mlb_feature_vector` signature and `_NON_FEATURE_MLB`. `_MODEL_THRESHOLDS = frozenset({1, 2})`. **Test count:** 9 changes in `test_run_mlb_paper_trade.py`, 7 changes in `test_t183_mlb_c3.py`. Shadow gate expected to PASS after re-train on VPS with updated script. | EL |

Adding an MLB equivalent there would couple two sport-specific data sources into a single method, violating the C1/C2 sport-specificity boundary. The clean split: PaperTrader's responsibility is ledger mutation under the GG-034 file-lock invariant; the resolution script's responsibility is fetching the sport-specific stat. `settle_position(position_id, actual_stat, *, stat_label="actual_pts", session_id=None)` is the new interface — it handles threshold comparison, direction-aware P&L formula (`size * (1 - ask) / ask` for wins, `-size` for losses), ledger mutation, and `_accumulate_daily_loss()`, all under `self._lock, self._file_lock()`. `stat_label` is a stored metadata field only — `actual_pts` for NBA backward compat, `actual_hits` for MLB. EXP-017 does not read this field (only `outcome` + `pnl`). **P&L formula:** YES win: `pnl = size * (1 - (mid + vig/2)) / (mid + vig/2)`; YES loss: `pnl = -size`. NO win: `pnl = size * (1 - ((1-mid) + vig/2)) / ((1-mid) + vig/2)`; NO loss: `pnl = -size`. Identical to the formula in `resolve_pending()` — verified by regression tests. **`run_resolve_mlb.py` (PIPE-05R) design decisions:** (1) BOT_ENV=paper guard at module import (sys.exit(1) otherwise). Rate limited at 0.6s/call (mirrors `mlb_ingestion.py`). All exceptions caught silently; returns None on any failure (position stays open for retry). (4) `fetch_actual_hits(player_id, game_date)` tries `game_type="R"` (Regular Season) first, then `game_type="P"` (Postseason) — mirrors NBA `resolve_pending()` Playoffs fallback pattern. (5) Future-date guard: only positions with `game_date < today` are eligible; future-dated positions are skipped silently. (6) API None → C6 warning + position stays open (idempotent: safe to rerun; will retry on next 06:30 run). (7) VPS accessibility caveat documented in docstring: 2026 MLB Stats API accessible from DigitalOcean; historical ≤2025 blocked (Fastly CDN, HTTP 406). **`deploy/cron.conf` naming correction (PIPE-05R → PIPE-05FX):** SESSION 81 added the feature refresh cron at 22:30 UTC with the comment label "PIPE-05R". This was incorrect — PIPE-05R by naming convention belongs to the resolution-only script (matching PIPE-03R = NBA resolution). Feature refresh is not a resolution pipeline; renamed to PIPE-05FX (FX = Feature eXtraction) in this session. PIPE-05R is now correctly assigned to `run_resolve_mlb.py` at 06:30 UTC. The per-player market loop passes `sp_override = sp_override_by_player.get(player_id)` to `_build_mlb_feature_vector`. **SP fetch latency:** ~10 unique SPs on a full MLB game night; each `fetch_pitcher_game_log` call takes ~0.6s (DEFAULT_REQUEST_DELAY_S); total ~6s overhead at PIPE-05 start. | 2026-06-24 | **[SESSION 82] T-187H COMPLETE — t=3 shadow gate re-evaluation: valid negative confirmed; GG-042 structurally deferred** | **Purpose of T-187H:** GG-042 registered t=3 (3+ hits, KXMLBHIT-...-4) as excluded from `_MODEL_THRESHOLDS` pending T-187G (live SP overlay). GG-042 resolution condition: "When `opp_sp_era_l5`, `opp_sp_k9_l5` have well-populated training rows for 3+ hit contexts AND the SP overlay provides discrimination at the t=3 base rate, re-run T-187B with t=3 included." T-187G is now COMPLETE; T-187H is the formal re-evaluation. **Shadow gate re-evaluation (local full 3-season feature matrix, 2026-06-24):** Ran the T-187B per-threshold pipeline independently for t=3 on `exp_output/MLB/mlb_feature_matrix.csv` (126,421 rows; holdout cutoff 2026-01-01). Results: train_n=101,400 rows at t=3; holdout_n=25,021 rows. null_rate=3.91%. Brier delta=**+0.0002 (FAIL** — required ≥0.001). ECE=0.0037 (PASS). AUC=**0.6141**. **t=3 shadow gate FAIL — same result as T-187B.** T-187G did not change the training data and therefore did not change the shadow gate outcome. **Root cause analysis — why the failure is structural:** (1) SP coverage: verified 99.9% of t=3 positive events (h≥3) have `opp_sp_is_known=1`. The GG-042 premise (poor SP coverage causing the failure) was DISPROVED. Coverage is already excellent; T-187G's live overlay addresses inference accuracy, not training data quality. (2) Score distribution analysis: p50=0.037 (≈ base rate). Only 1.9% of holdout predictions exceed 0.08 (≈ Kalshi market price). Even at AUC=0.61, the model cannot shift predictions meaningfully above the market price — it clusters around the 3.9% base rate. (3) Brier delta ceiling at 3.9% base rate: at null_rate=0.039, the maximum possible Brier delta is ~p*(1-p) = 0.037. Any LR model with AUC=0.61 will produce a small absolute delta even with perfect calibration. This is a fundamental information-theoretic limit, not a feature quality issue. (4) Edge would almost never fire: only 487 of 25,021 holdout predictions exceed 0.08 (1.9%) — model_p < market_price_implied = negative edge for 98.1% of bets. **Conclusion:** The t=3 exclusion is structurally correct and not resolvable via SP feature improvement. A richer model (non-linear, additional SP feature interactions, pitcher style matchup data) or different market dynamics (Kalshi price drops below 5–6%) are the only paths to t=3 activation. Neither is available at current system state. **No code changes.** `THRESHOLDS = [1, 2]` and `_EXCLUDED_THRESHOLDS = [3]` in `train_mlb_model.py` unchanged. `_MODEL_THRESHOLDS = frozenset({1, 2})` in `run_mlb_paper_trade.py` and `run_mlb_calib_monitor.py` unchanged. **GG-042 status updated to DEFERRED — structural.** Test suite unchanged at 1790/1790. | EL |

| 2026-06-25 | **[SESSION 84] T-189 COMPLETE — PIPE-05 three-concern remediation: GAME_DATE=tomorrow, market date filter, ensure_persisted(), hydrate fix, PIPE-05FX cache preservation; 29 new tests, 1819 total green** | **Three operational failures surfaced in PIPE-05 session-83 first run (23:31 UTC 2026-06-24) addressed in this session. (1) Contamination guard fires every night.** Root cause: `GAME_DATE = datetime.now(tz=UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d")` — today's date. At 23:31 UTC (7:31pm ET), east-coast MLB games are "Live" → `_is_mlb_game_in_progress(today_iso)` returns True → abort, 0 bets. Fix: `GAME_DATE = (now_utc + timedelta(days=1)).date()` — T1 night-before baseline design. Tomorrow's games are always "Preview" at 23:30 UTC. **Architectural decision:** Market date filter added alongside GAME_DATE fix. Without it, active Kalshi markets for today's in-progress games could pass through the markets_by_player loop even after fixing the contamination guard (Kalshi returns all open markets regardless of date). Filter: `if _ticker_to_game_date(m.ticker) == GAME_DATE_ISO:` ensures only markets for tomorrow's slate are evaluated. (2) `paper_portfolio_mlb.json` missing.** Root cause: `PaperTrader._load_or_init()` only writes to disk on first `_save()` call (triggered by bet placement); contamination guard aborted before any bets, so file was never created. Fix design: adding the disk write to `_load_or_init()` itself was rejected — `_reload_locked()` inside `resolve_pending()` re-reads from disk under the file lock, which would overwrite any direct `_state` mutations (pattern used by 10 unit tests, which all failed). | 2026-06-24 | **[SESSION 82] T-187F COMPLETE — MLB calibration monitor (PIPE-05C): derive_temperature design, T_global strategy, non-monotonic miscalibration finding, 35 new tests, 1772 total green** | **Script design: `scripts/run_mlb_calib_monitor.py`.** Reads `paper_portfolio_mlb.json` closed positions; filters to active thresholds `_MODEL_THRESHOLDS = frozenset({1, 2})` (t=3 excluded per GG-042); computes ECE per threshold; derives recommended calibration temperature; reports reliability buckets and anti-predictive check. Does NOT write to `calibration.py` — temperature changes require ADR. Exit codes: 0=CALIBRATED or insufficient data, 1=MISCALIBRATED (ECE>0.05), 2=ANTI_PREDICTIVE. **Temperature derivation (`derive_temperature`): `scipy.optimize.minimize_scalar` on log-loss of `sigmoid(logit(P)/T)`, bounds=(0.3, 8.0).** Mirrors `scripts/derive_calibration.py` exactly. T>1 shrinks (overconfident model); T<1 sharpens (underconfident); T=1 is identity. Gate: `MIN_N_DIAGNOSE=50` positions required for derivation (falls back to identity T=1.0). **T_global strategy (key design decision):** Temperature is derived on the combined all-threshold dataset, then the SAME T_global is applied to both per-threshold ECE diagnoses. This avoids underfitting on per-threshold subsets: at ~50 total positions, each threshold contributes ~25 positions — insufficient for stable Platt fitting. The per-threshold ECE diagnosis answers "does this threshold have a calibration problem at the current T?" not "what is the optimal T for this threshold?". Per-threshold temperature fitting would require ≥50 per-threshold (i.e., ≥100 total) — deferred until data volume justifies it. **ECE formula:** `sum_b (n_b/n) * |mean_pred_b − mean_actual_b|` over 5 reliability buckets [(0.5,0.6),(0.6,0.7),(0.7,0.8),(0.8,0.9),(0.9,1.01)]. ADR-0027 shadow gate threshold ≤0.05. **Anti-predictive check:** calibrated edge = `PC − MIP − 0.07 × MIP × (1 − MIP)`; compares win rate of edge>0 cohort vs edge≤0 cohort. Raises exit code 2 if edge>0 wins at lower rate (mirrors GG-037 diagnosis logic for NBA). **PIPE-05C cron entry:** `10 14 * * 1` — Mondays 14:10 UTC (10 minutes after model freshness check). Exit codes captured in `logs/mlb_calib_monitor.log`. **Key finding during test development: temperature scaling cannot fix non-monotonic miscalibration.** A uniformly overconfident model (all positions predict p=0.90, actual win rate 0.55) IS fully corrected by temperature scaling — T shifts P_cal to match the average win rate, collapsing ECE to near-zero regardless of input model_p. This made naive overconfident test fixtures produce exit code 0 instead of 1. Non-monotonic miscalibration — two groups with inverted calibration errors (Group A severely underconfident, Group B severely overconfident) — CANNOT be fixed by a single multiplicative temperature because the two errors cannot be simultaneously corrected. Test fixtures required: Group A (p=0.51, win_rate=0.85) + Group B (p=0.99, win_rate=0.15). At T=8.0 (max bound): Group A P_cal=0.501 lands in [0.5,0.6) bucket, Group B P_cal=0.640 lands in [0.6,0.7) bucket — different buckets, ECE ≈ 0.43 >> 0.05. All MIP=0.05 ensures all calibrated edges remain positive (anti-predictive check skipped → exit 1 not exit 2). **Test coverage (35 tests in 10 classes):** `TestLoadPositions` (4) — skips duplicates/non-WIN-LOSS/None model_p, reads mid_price_at_entry; `TestToArrays` (6) — directional frame YES/NO P/MIP transforms (uses processed pos dict with "mip" key, not raw JSON "mid_price_at_entry"); `TestDeriveTemperature` (4) — identity at T=1, overconfident >1, underconfident <1, bounds respected; `TestComputeEce` (5) — zero, single bucket, multi-bucket, perfect calibration, boundary 0.9-1.0; `TestDiagnoseThreshold` (4) — insufficient data (exit None/False), sufficient PASS, sufficient FAIL; `TestMainNoFile` (1); `TestMainNoPositions` (1); `TestMainInsufficientData` (2) — deferred at n<50; `TestMainCalibrated` (3) — passes at n≥50 PASS; `TestMainMiscalibrated` (4) — non-monotonic FAIL on combined thresholds; `TestMainAntiPredictive` (1) — edge>0 cohort losing at higher rate. **Suite: 1737 → 1772/1772** (0 failures, 1 skipped). | EL |

| 2026-06-27 | **[SESSION 90 / T-194] `mark_aged_open()` design: in-place tagging within `open_positions`, NOT movement to a separate list** | `PaperTrader.mark_aged_open()` adds `aged_open=True`, `aged_open_reason`, `aged_open_at` fields to the position dict in-place within `self._state["open_positions"]`. It does NOT move the position to `closed_positions` or a separate aged list. **Reason:** `_ledger_bankroll()` derives `open_cost` by summing `open_positions[*]["size_usd"]`. Moving a position out of `open_positions` would credit that capital back to the bankroll — incorrect, because the money is still committed (the paper bet exists; we just cannot resolve it). This would overstate available capital and allow subsequent over-sizing. **Boundary condition (>48h rule):** Age is computed from midnight of `game_date` in UTC (not game start time, which is unavailable at resolve time). Only positions with `game_date < today` are in the resolution loop; an additional `age_hours > 48` check against datetime.now(UTC) ensures the midnight-boundary case cannot trigger early. Fixed-noon UTC in boundary tests prevents test failures at the exact midnight transition. **C6 behavior:** `mark_aged_open()` emits `paper_position_aged_open` WARNING to C7 audit log. The position remains in `open_positions` and is excluded from future resolution attempts (caller responsibility — runner skips `aged_open=True` positions). | EL |

| 2026-06-27 | **[SESSION 90 / T-195] Permissive-with-logging pattern for silent exception handlers in MLB contamination guards** | Two functions had bare `except Exception: pass`: `_is_mlb_game_in_progress()` in `run_mlb_paper_trade.py` and `_fetch_live_game_pks()` in `run_t2_mlb_sameday.py`. **Design decision:** Both are intentionally PERMISSIVE on failure (return `False` / empty set = assume no games in progress = allow pipeline to proceed). This is correct for a contamination guard: false-negative (treating a live game as not-live) is the safe fail mode. A false-positive (treating a non-live schedule as live) would block all bets unnecessarily. **Only change:** Added optional `logger: AuditLogger | None = None` and `session_id: str | None = None` parameters. When logger is provided, API failures emit `mlb_schedule_api_failed` WARNING to C6 instead of swallowing silently. The permissive return behavior is unchanged. **Why not raise?** Raising would convert an API transient failure into a pipeline abort, blocking all bets. The guard's purpose is to prevent stale positions during live games — if the schedule API is down, the conservative path (allow bets) is correct, but the failure should be visible in the audit log. | EL |

| 2026-06-26 | **[SESSION 92 / T-200] Inning-aware `ab_remaining` formula — eliminates ±14–25% Binomial probability error in MLB T4** | **Root cause of GG-007 blocker:** Old formula `max(0, round(3.7 - ab_so_far))` ignored current inning entirely. ±1 AB error in `ab_remaining` propagates to ±14–25% error in `p_hits_gte_threshold()` via Binomial CDF — 3–6× larger than the 4% MIN_EDGE threshold. The inning field was already live in `MLBLiveBatterState.inning` (fetched from ESPN, stored at line 259) but was never used. **Fix: `_compute_ab_remaining(ab_so_far, inning, batting_slot=0) -> int`.** Takes the conservative minimum of two signals: (1) `ab_from_inning = expected_total × (innings_remaining / 9.0)` where `innings_remaining = max(0.0, 9.0 - inning + 0.5)` — inning-fraction cap; (2) `ab_from_total = max(0.0, expected_total - ab_so_far)` — expected-total-minus-used. Returns `max(0, round(min(ab_from_inning, ab_from_total)))`. **`_AB_BY_SLOT` dict** `{1:4.2, 2:4.1, 3:4.0, 4:3.9, 5:3.7, 6:3.6, 7:3.4, 8:3.2, 9:3.1}` — slot-specific expected ABs; falls back to `AB_PER_GAME_DEFAULT=3.7` for unknown slot. `_AB_BY_SLOT` already defined in T2 (`run_t2_mlb_sameday.py:103-106`); added to T4 for consistency. **`_load_t2_batting_slots() -> dict[int, int]`:** loads batting slots from today's most recent `t2_mlb_YYYYMMDD_HHMM.json` (date-filtered to prevent yesterday's lineup). Falls back gracefully (returns `{}`) if no T2 output exists for today. Batting slots are optional — T4 runs without them, falling back to the 3.7 default. **Extra-innings known limitation:** `innings_remaining` clamps to 0 for inning > 9 → `ab_remaining=0` → T4 stops all evaluations in extra innings. Conservative and acceptable for paper trading. **Changes:** `_compute_ab_remaining()` function, `_AB_BY_SLOT` constant, `_load_t2_batting_slots()` function, `_run_cycle()` signature updated, `batting_slots` loaded in `main()`. 22 new tests added (2 constants tests, 13 `_compute_ab_remaining`, 5 `_load_t2_batting_slots`, 2 `_run_cycle` integration). Suite: 2051 → 2070, all pass. | EL |

| 2026-06-26 | **[SESSION 93] T4 MLB evaluation throughput gap — structural analysis, three root causes identified** | **Design target:** ~1,000 evaluations per game (CLAUDE.md Hard Rule 9). **Simulation output (MC, SESSION 93):** 477 evaluations per day across 15 games = 31.8/game. **Gap is structural across three layers: (1) ESPN API returns current-batter state, not full box score.** `_espn_poll_once_mlb()` builds `MLBLiveBatterState` from the ESPN live game endpoint which surfaces the current play situation (who's at the plate now). It does NOT return cumulative stats for all 18 batters who have appeared. Per cycle, ~1 batter state per game is available → 15 games × 1 batter × 2 thresholds × 480 cycles = ~14,400 raw evaluations vs design ~259,200 (15 games × 18 batters × 2 thresholds × 480 cycles). Fix requires `_espn_poll_once_mlb()` to query the ESPN box score endpoint (separate from the scoreboard endpoint) and build `MLBLiveBatterState` for every player who has appeared, not just the current batter. This is a targeted code change that unblocks T-203 from being calibrated at design throughput. **(2) Decision tree is flat — no sub-bands.** Current MLB T4 routes every evaluation through one condition: `net_edge > 0`. The full T4 architecture (ADR-0029/ADR-0030) defines four sub-bands (T4-PROJ, T4-ARITH, T4-EVENT, T4-FLOW) with phase-conditional Kelly schedules. These are Phase 8 blocked. The MLB T4 is a calibration vehicle, not the full decision tree. **(3) `already_open` guard caps fires at one per player-threshold pair per game.** Maximum fires per game = tracked player-threshold pairs = ~53 players × 1.8 thresholds ≈ 95. This is correct risk behavior (no double-sizing). The ~1,000 target is evaluation volume, not fires — fires of 10–50/game is the intentional design outcome after filtering. **Register as Gap 1 for T-203 context:** The JSONL corpus from T-202 dry-run will reflect the current single-batter-per-cycle throughput, not the design throughput. T-203 must calibrate MIN_EDGE_T4 against ACTUAL throughput (correct), not design throughput. If Gap 1 is fixed before T-203 ADR is written, T-203 gets a higher-fidelity distribution. Decision on whether to fix Gap 1 before or alongside T-203 is deferred to the next session. | EL |
| 2026-06-26 | **[SESSION 94] Strategic framing — four structural observations documented as ongoing factors** | External strategic read-through of the project surfaced four structural observations. All confirmed substantively correct. Documented as ongoing factors, not resolved points. **(1) Market efficiency is context-dependent.** NBA Finals player props are among the most liquid, most-watched prediction markets that exist — priced by sharp money, sports media, and high public volume simultaneously. The anti-predictive finding (GG-037) was produced in worst-case efficiency conditions. MLB mid-week regular season markets are structurally different: lower public interest, thinner books, slower price discovery. G6 MLB evidence is not "more data" — it is data from a different, less-efficient market regime. This reframes GG-004 (EXP-017): the commercial question has never been tested in the environment the strategy is designed to exploit. **(2) T4's alpha hypothesis is structurally distinct from T1's and requires separate commercial validation.** T1's thesis: "calibrated historical model beats pregame market pricing." T4's thesis: "market is slow to update to live game state; bot can get there first." These are different alpha sources. EXP-017 (planned) tests T1. No experiment is registered for T4's independent commercial validation. Even if T1 fails EXP-017, T4 could independently work via microstructure edge. Even if T1 passes, T4 might not. T4 requires its own validation track. Registered separately as GG-046. **(3) Kalshi market depth creates a capital ceiling independent of bankroll math.** Kelly criterion assumes infinite market depth. Kalshi KXMLBHIT player prop lines are thin (estimated $500–$2,000 per market). At $1K paper with 25% Kelly, position sizing is $50–$250/bet — within depth. At $10K+ bankroll, full Kelly sizing would move the market against the bot — degrading or eliminating edge before the position fills. The maximum effective bankroll is constrained by market depth, not bankroll formula or Kelly fraction calibration. This ceiling is absent from governance documentation. Registered as GG-045. **(4) The methodology is the product.** Prediction markets provide high-frequency, binary-resolved calibration feedback — uniquely clean for testing probabilistic thinking (unambiguous resolution, short feedback loops, no confounding). The trading bot is the calibration regime; the methodology (build → forecast → validate → iterate against resolved binary outcomes) is the transferable asset. P&L is the score; the methodology is the value. Preserved as a framing note for post-G6 strategic review. | DS |

| 2026-06-27 | **[SESSION 95] T4 MLB deployment architecture — 3-layer defense (PIPE-08 + startup self-heal + systemd)** | **Problem:** Under the old cron design, if the VPS rebooted or T4 crashed after 13:00 UTC, T4 remained dead for the rest of the day. The `timeout 43200` cron wrapper prevented clean restart; cron would not re-trigger until 13:00 UTC the next calendar day. This meant any mid-session failure = entire evening's games unmonitored. **3-layer defense implemented:** (1) **PIPE-08** (`deploy/cron.conf`, `0 8 * * *`): schedule validation at 08:00 UTC daily; computes optimal startup time based on that day's first pitch minus 30 minutes. Guards against early-game miss. (2) **Startup self-heal** (`run_t4_mlb_live.py`): on startup, if current UTC time is past the PIPE-08 optimal start, the process immediately loads today's games and begins evaluating without waiting for next cycle. Crontab timing becomes advisory; the process self-corrects. (3) **systemd `Restart=on-failure`** (`deploy/t4-mlb.service`): if the T4 process exits with a non-zero code for any reason (crash, OOM, unhandled exception), systemd restarts it within 60 seconds. `RestartSec=60`. `WantedBy=multi-user.target` ensures the service starts on VPS boot without operator intervention. **Date-rollover refresh:** 24/7 systemd service monitors `date.today()` in the main loop. At UTC midnight, refreshes `game_ids`, pregame context (both player dicts), batting slots, and T4 band budget. Logs the rollover event with game count and budget. **Files created:** `deploy/t4-mlb.service`, `deploy/t4-mlb.timer`, `deploy/install_systemd_t4_mlb.sh`. **`deploy/cron.conf`:** T4 MLB cron line commented out with "REPLACED BY SYSTEMD — SESSION 95" notice. **VPS deploy:** `install_systemd_t4_mlb.sh` executed; systemd confirmed active, `Restart=on-failure` confirmed via `systemctl show`. Old PID 696748/696749 (cron-started) killed after duplicate process detected. Systemd PID 698979 confirmed running. Commits `982ef4f` (self-heal) + `9f5b869` (systemd + rollover). | EL |

| 2026-06-28 | **[SESSION 96 / T-205] Kalshi KXMLBHIT ticker off-by-one: `_ticker_to_game_date` fixed to add 1 day** | **Root cause of T1 MLB "0 players matched":** `_ticker_to_game_date()` in `run_mlb_paper_trade.py` extracted the `{DD}` day from Kalshi tickers and returned it as the game date. **Kalshi convention: markets open the day before the game; `{DD}` is the market creation date, not the game date.** Ticker `KXMLBHIT-26JUN271310NYYBOS-...` = game on June 27, ticker says June 26. The filter `if _ticker_to_game_date(m.ticker) == GAME_DATE_ISO` always produced `"2026-06-26" == "2026-06-27"` → False for all 149 markets. Every PIPE-05 run since MLB-G1 gate returned 0 matched players. Zero T1 bets placed. **Fix:** Return `(kalshi_date + timedelta(days=1))` from `_ticker_to_game_date()`. Production code correctly ignores the YY field and uses `datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc).year` — no change needed. **Test files updated:** `tests/test_run_mlb_paper_trade.py` (3 assertions: `+1d` offsets) + `tests/test_t189_mlb_pipe05_fixes.py` (local copy + 5 assertions: `+1d` offsets, `%d%b%Y` year logic). | 2026-06-29 | **[SESSION 97 / T-206a] Kalshi KXMLBHIT ticker format correction: `_ticker_to_game_date` rewritten to extract GAME_DD directly** | **S96 fix was wrong.** S96 (T-205) assumed the KXMLBHIT ticker format was `{CREATE_DD}{MON}{YY}{HHMM}` and that `+timedelta(days=1)` applied because Kalshi always creates markets exactly 1 day before the game. This was wrong on two counts: (1) **The `{YY}` field is actually `{GAME_DD}`** — the 3rd 2-digit group in `KXMLBHIT-26JUN291840PITPHI` is `29`, the game day-of-month, not a 2-digit year. (2) Kalshi creates markets 1–3 days early (observed: `KXMLBHIT-26JUN291840PITPHI` created June 26, game June 29 — 3 days early). The S96 fix (`CREATE_DD + 1`) happened to produce the correct result for the 1-day-early case but returned June 27 for June 29 games (created June 26), matching 0 markets. **Real fix:** Extract GAME_DD directly using regex `r"-[0-9]{2}([A-Z]{3})([0-9]{2})[0-9]{4}"` (MON=group 1, GAME_DD=group 2) and construct date as `f"{m.group(2)}{m.group(1)}{year}"`. No `timedelta` needed. **Ticker format confirmed:** `KXMLBHIT-{CREATE_DD}{MON}{GAME_DD}{HHMM}{AWAYTEAM}{HOMETEAM}-...` — CREATE_DD=market creation date, GAME_DD=actual game date, both sharing same MON. Year inferred from `datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc).year` (MLB runs March–October; no cross-year risk). **Root cause of root cause:** S96 diagnosed from the docstring example (`KXMLBHIT-26JUN271310NYYBOS` → S96 read `YY=27` as 2027, ignored it, inferred game=June 26+1=June 27). The example happened to have GAME_DD=27=CREATE_DD+1, so the wrong formula produced the right answer for that case. S97 caught the discrepancy via live log: `KXMLBHIT-26JUN291905DETNYY` (pregame June 29) was being parsed as June 27 (CREATE_DD=26+1), not June 29 (GAME_DD=29). **Tests updated:** Tickers in `test_run_mlb_paper_trade.py` and `test_t189_mlb_pipe05_fixes.py` corrected to use proper `{CREATE_DD}{MON}{GAME_DD}{HHMM}` format; 3 new test cases added (multi-day early creation, ticker from June 23 for June 25 game). Suite: 2113/2113. Committed `d5289a0`. VPS at `d5289a0`. **GG-047 RESOLVED (real fix).** | EL |

No recovery was attempted. **Fix:** Added `_RETRY_DELAYS_S: tuple[int, ...] = (5, 10, 30)`. `_fetch_mlb_hits()` iterates `(*_RETRY_DELAYS_S, None)` — 4 total attempts (3 retries). Each transient exception (`HTTPError`, connection error, timeout) triggers a retry with escalating delay (5s → 10s → 30s), with stderr logging per attempt. Final attempt gives up and returns `None`. Date-not-found path (200 response, no matching date in splits) returns `None` immediately — not transient, no retry. **Run-level alert in `_resolve_pool()`:** After the resolution loop: if `pending` is non-empty but `len(resolved) == 0`, both `logger.warning("PIPE-05R", "zero_resolved_with_eligible_positions", ...)` and a stderr print are emitted, explicitly citing GG-048. This ensures a full-outage run is visible in logs rather than silent. **Tests:** 2113/2113 pass. GG-048 RESOLVED. | EL |

The same market was re-bet on every cycle for the entire game, producing an average of 17.5 bets per unique kalshi_ticker (max: 65×, min: 1×). **Corrected data (73 independent markets):** Source: `paper_portfolio_mlb_t4.json`, first occurrence of each `kalshi_ticker` only. June 27–28 game nights; 2 game days only. n=73 unique markets with resolved outcomes. Win rate: 27/73 = 37.0%. Net P&L (first-occurrence bets only): **-$0.18**.
